Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

!((x / (y / z)) = 10)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#rank_1: bool
  find x#rank_2: bool
  find x#rank_3: bool
  find x#rank_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#rank_4),or([!(x#rank_3),or([!(x#rank_2),or([!(x#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#rank_1: bool
  find y#rank_2: bool
  find y#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(y#rank_3),and([!(y#rank_2),or([!(y#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#rank_1: bool
  find z#rank_2: bool
  find z#rank_3: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#rank_3),or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

!((x / (y / z)) = 10),
true,
or([!(y#rank_3),and([!(y#rank_2);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
or([!(z#rank_3),or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
!((x / (y / z)) = 10),
true,
or([!(z#rank_3),or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(or([!(y#rank_3),and([!(y#rank_2);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

!((x / (y / z)) = 10),
true,
or([!(z#rank_3),or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
   ~~> select_asserted_boolean ([("SAT", 18000)])
!((x / (y / z)) = 10),
true
new SAT encoding decisions:
  assert(or([!(z#rank_3),or([!(z#rank_2),and([!(z#rank_1);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])) using tseitin

--

x
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20])

--

y
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5])

--

z
   ~~> integer_decision_representation_unsigned ([("SAT", 9500)])
SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6])

--

(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]) / SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))
   ~~> div_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
{SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6])) @ SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]) != 0}

--

SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]) != 0
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*z#rank_1, 2*z#rank_2, 4*z#rank_3) NotEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 3, structure: BoundedBinary { lower: 0, upper: 6 } }]

--

(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / {SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6])) @ __0})
   ~~> bubble_up ([("Bubble", 8800)])
{(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))) @ __0}

--

{(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))) @ __0} = 10
   ~~> bubble_up ([("Bubble", 8800)])
{(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))) = 10 @ __0}

--

{(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))) = 10 @ __0}
   ~~> expand_bubble ([("Bubble", 8900)])
and([(SATInt(Rank([(5, 20)]), [x#rank_1,x#rank_2,x#rank_3,x#rank_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) / SafeDiv(SATInt(Rank([(0, 5)]), [y#rank_1,y#rank_2,y#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 5]), SATInt(Rank([(0, 6)]), [z#rank_1,z#rank_2,z#rank_3;int(1..)] [0, 6]))) = 10,__0;int(1..)])

--

   ~~> div_to_bubble ([("Bubble", 6000)])
